Yeah… I think so. I mean, I don’t want to sound dramatic, because it’s not like I hate those things now or anything. I just… don’t really do them.

I’m sorry, I don’t know if that explains it right.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling down or losing interest in things you used to enjoy most of the time, almost every day?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. I don’t know if I’d call it “down” all the time, exactly, but I just don’t really… do anything anymore. I mostly sit in my room, and I’ve been wearing the same clothes more than I probably should.\n\nI used to knit and journal and go for walks, and now I just can’t make myself start. When my mom asks me what’s wrong, I barely have the energy to answer her. I’m sorry if that sounds bad… but yes, it’s been happening a lot.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling like you’re not good enough, or blamed yourself for things more than usual?\n\nPatient: Um… I don’t know. Not really, I guess? I mean, I get frustrated with myself for not doing more, but I don’t sit there thinking I’m a terrible person or anything. At least… I don’t think so.\n\nIs that the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling more tired than usual, or like you don’t have any energy?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. I mean, I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is, but it’s like I don’t have the energy to start anything.\n\nMostly I just stay in my room, and I’ll realize I’ve been in the same clothes again. I used to knit, or write in my journal, or go for walks, and now even those feel like… too much. And when my mom asks me what’s wrong, sometimes I can barely answer her.\n\nI’m sorry, is that what you mean by tired?\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your ability to focus or make decisions lately?\n\nPatient: Um… I’m not sure. I mean, I guess I zone out sometimes, but I don’t know if it’s more than normal.\n\nDecisions… maybe I put things off, but I haven’t really noticed a big change, I don’t think. Sorry, I’m not sure if that answers it right.\n\nDoctor: Have you found that activities you used to enjoy, like knitting or walking, don’t give you the same pleasure anymore?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. I mean, I don’t want to sound dramatic, because it’s not like I hate those things now or anything. I just… don’t really do them.\n\nLike knitting or journaling, or even going for a walk, they used to feel nice, and now it’s more like I can’t make myself start. I mostly just sit in my room, sometimes still in the same clothes, and then my mom asks what’s wrong and I barely have the energy to answer her.\n\nSo… yeah, I guess they don’t really feel the same anymore. Is that what you mean?\n\nDoctor: Would you say you’ve been feeling down or depressed most days, even if it’s not extremely intense?\n\nPatient: I… I don’t know if I’d call it depressed. Maybe that sounds too strong.\n\nI’m not exactly happy, but it’s more like I feel kind of flat or heavy a lot of the time. Some days are okay, I guess. I don’t want to exaggerate it. But… yeah, I guess most days I don’t feel very good.\n\nIs that the kind of thing you mean?\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. When my mom asks me what’s wrong, I barely have the energy to answer her. I’m sorry if that sounds bad… but yes, it’s been happening a lot.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling like you’re not good enough, or blamed yourself for things more than usual?\n\nPatient: Um… I don’t know. At least… I don’t think so.\n\nIs that the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling more tired than usual, or like you don’t have any energy?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. I mean, I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is, but it’s like I don’t have the energy to start anything.\n\nMostly I just stay in my room, and I’ll realize I’ve been in the same clothes again. I used to knit, or write in my journal, or go for walks, and now even those feel like… too much. And when my mom asks me what’s wrong, sometimes I can barely answer her.\n\nI’m sorry, is that what you mean by tired?\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your ability to focus or make decisions lately?\n\nPatient: Um… I’m not sure. I mean, I don’t want to sound dramatic, because it’s not like I hate those things now or anything. I just… don’t really do them.\n\nLike knitting or journaling, or even going for a walk, they used to feel nice, and now it’s more like I can’t make myself start. I mostly just sit in my room, sometimes still in the same clothes, and then my mom asks what’s wrong and I barely have the energy to answer her.\n\nSo… yeah, I guess they don’t really feel the same anymore. Is that what you mean?\n\nDoctor: Would you say you’ve been feeling down or depressed most days, even if it’s not extremely intense?\n\nPatient: I… I don’t know if I’d call it depressed. Maybe that sounds too strong.\n\nI’m not exactly happy, but it’s more like I feel kind of flat or heavy a lot of the time. Some days are okay, I guess. I don’t want to exaggerate it. But… yeah, I guess most days I don’t feel very good.\n\nIs that the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: Would you say that most days you feel a depressed mood, even if it's not very strong?\n\nPatient: I think… maybe? I mostly sit in my room, and I’ve been wearing the same clothes more than I probably should.\n\nI used to knit and journal and go for walks, and now I just can’t make myself start. When my mom asks me what’s wrong, I barely have the energy to answer her. I’m sorry if that sounds bad… but yes, it’s been happening a lot.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling like you’re not good enough, or blamed yourself for things more than usual?\n\nPatient: Um… I don’t know. Not really, I guess? I mean, I get frustrated with myself for not doing more, but I don’t sit there thinking I’m a terrible person or anything. I mean, I guess I zone out sometimes, but I don’t know if it’s more than normal.\n\nDecisions… maybe I put things off, but I haven’t really noticed a big change, I don’t think. Sorry, I’m not sure if that answers it right.\n\nDoctor: Have you found that activities you used to enjoy, like knitting or walking, don’t give you the same pleasure anymore?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. I mean, I don’t want to sound dramatic, because it’s not like I hate those things now or anything. I just… don’t really do them.\n\nLike knitting or journaling, or even going for a walk, they used to feel nice, and now it’s more like I can’t make myself start. I mostly just sit in my room, sometimes still in the same clothes, and then my mom asks what’s wrong and I barely have the energy to answer her.\n\nSo… yeah, I guess they don’t really feel the same anymore. Is that what you mean?\n\nDoctor: Would you say you’ve been feeling down or depressed most days, even if it’s not extremely intense?\n\nPatient: I… I don’t know if I’d call it depressed. Maybe that sounds too strong.\n\nI’m not exactly happy, but it’s more like I feel kind of flat or heavy a lot of the time. Some days are okay, I guess. I don’t want to exaggerate it. But… yeah, I guess most days I don’t feel very good.\n\nIs that the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: Would you say that most days you feel a depressed mood, even if it's not very strong?\n\nPatient: I think… maybe? Does that count?\n\nDoctor: Would you say that most days you feel a depressed mood, even if it's not very strong?\n\nPatient: I’m… not totally sure how to answer it differently. If that counts as depressed mood, then maybe yes.\n\nDoctor: Have you had a period of time where you felt this way nearly every day for at least two weeks in a row?\n\nPatient: I’m not completely sure… I don’t really keep track of days very well.\n\nIt feels like it’s been around a lot, but saying “nearly every day for two weeks” sounds so specific. I don’t want to say yes if I’m just remembering it wrong.\n\nIt has felt like that a lot, like most days I’m kind of down, but when you say almost every day for a couple weeks or more, I don’t know if I can be certain.
